## Summary: Code-Review-and-Quality Skill Structure

**Location:** The source of truth is a cached marketplace artifact at `/Users/anton/.claude/plugins/marketplaces/addy-agent-skills/skills/code-review-and-quality/SKILL.md` (348 lines). This is referenced by a workflow rule at `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/code-review.md` but the actual skill definition is not yet in the agent-skills source repos — it's distributed via the marketplace.

**Internal Structure:**

The SKILL.md is organized as follows:

1. **Overview** — philosophy of "improve code health continuously"
2. **When to Use** — entry conditions
3. **The Five-Axis Review** (four separate subsections):
   - Correctness
   - Readability & Simplicity  
   - Architecture
   - Security
   - Performance
4. **Change Sizing** — PR sizing guidance + splitting strategies
5. **Change Descriptions** — commit message standards
6. **Review Process** — five-step methodology (context → tests → implementation → categorize → verify)
7. **Multi-Model Review Pattern** — agent collaboration
8. **Dead Code Hygiene** — explicit patterns for cleanup
9. **Review Speed** — response-time SLAs
10. **Handling Disagreements** — conflict resolution hierarchy
11. **Honesty in Review** — anti-patterns in reviewing
12. **Dependency Discipline** — before adding dependencies
13. **The Review Checklist** — markdown-formatted template
14. **Common Rationalizations** — myth-busting
15. **Red Flags** — anti-patterns to watch for
16. **Verification** — post-review steps

**Tone:** Pragmatic, direct, conversational. Includes canonical examples and explicit anti-patterns.

---

**Existing Comment-Rule Coverage:**

Currently, **Readability & Simplicity** (section 36, line 46) includes only:
- "Would comments help clarify non-obvious intent? (But don't comment obvious code.)"
- Mention of dead code artifacts like `// removed` comments (line 47)

No existing rules about comment cross-references (README sections, ticket IDs, commit SHAs). The skill assumes comments should clarify *intent*, not reference external documents.

---

**Existing "Smell"-Style Heuristics:**

The skill includes several "delete the workaround" patterns:

1. **Line 44:** "Could this be done in fewer lines?" — implies deleting unnecessary code
2. **Line 45:** "Are abstractions earning their complexity? (Don't generalize until the third use case)" — delete premature generalization
3. **Lines 207-224 (Dead Code Hygiene section):** Explicit "ask before deleting" pattern for orphaned code
4. **Line 47:** Calls out `// removed` comments as dead-code artifacts to spot and clean up

The heuristics are phrased as *questions* within bullet lists, not as standalone subsections.

---

**Natural Landing Zone for New Heuristics:**

The two new rules would naturally extend the **Readability & Simplicity** section (lines 36–48). This section is already focused on code clarity and self-documentation. Specifically:

**After line 46** (the existing comment guidance):
```
- Would comments help clarify non-obvious intent? (But don't comment obvious code.)
- [INSERT: Comment-as-smell rule here]
- [INSERT: Doc cross-reference discipline rule here]
```

**Rationale:** This section already discusses comment quality and dead-code artifacts. Both new rules are about *comment discipline*: one about when comments signal a design problem, the other about which external references comments should avoid. They slot naturally as bullet refinements to "readability", not as new major sections.

---

**Status in Workflow Pack (ABA-266):**

The skill exists in the marketplace (actively versioned) but the source truth is distributed. The workflow rule at `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/code-review.md` references it as a callable skill with fallback logic: "If the skill is not available, perform a five-axis diff review manually." This suggests the code-review-and-quality skill definition may not yet be checked into agent-skills-workflow as a local skill. The rule acts as the "gate" (line 1: "This rule applies to every issue… the following steps must run in order"), while the skill is the detailed executor.

**Recommendation for adding heuristics:** Since the skill is distributed via marketplace, updating it requires modifying the source in the addy-agent-skills plugin or equivalent source of truth (not yet visible in your /src/ directories). The workflow rule (code-review.md) is the *integration point* but not the skill authorship location.
```

> AGENT

Exploration found two important things that simplify the plan:

1. **The `code-review-and-quality` skill source isn't in your control** — it lives in the marketplace cache (`~/.claude/plugins/marketplaces/addy-agent-skills/.../SKILL.md`). Editing it would be lost on next sync. The two heuristics belong in **your** workflow pack, not the upstream skill.
2. **`GOVERNANCE.md` already has a `Comments` always-on section** indexed by the SessionStart hook. The two new heuristics (comment-as-smell, no `README §N` from code comments) are both comment-discipline rules — they slot directly into that section as new bullets. No new file needed for Issue 2.

That collapses both issues into the same repo (`agent-skills-workflow`) and removes the need to fork a marketplace skill. I'll skip the Plan-agent step — exploration was decisive — and write the plan now.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/anton/.claude/plans/eventual-cooking-flute.md",
  "content": "# Plan: capture stacked-PR learnings into agent-skills-workflow\n\n## Context\n\nABA-299 was delivered as a 4-PR Graphite stack rather than the usual push-to-main. That run surfaced two reusable patterns the workflow pack does not yet capture:\n\n1. **Handling reviewer comments across a stacked PR series.** Fetching comments via `gh api`, triaging which layer owns each one, amending bottom-up with `git commit --amend`, propagating with `gt restack`, and force-pushing the stack with `gt submit --stack`. Plus the small but real recovery move for the Edit tool's \"file has been modified\" error — re-read, re-apply, don't blind-retry.\n2. **Two code-comment heuristics observed by the reviewer.** \"Comment-as-smell\" (when the only justification for a special case is a multi-line explanatory comment, prefer deleting the special case — canonical example: the `_INT_ONLY_FIELDS` constant in `drain-cycle/drain_cycle/limits.py` from this run). And \"no `README §N` references from code comments\" (a code comment that says `see README §3` couples two artefacts that evolve independently; the link rots silently when README is restructured).\n\nABA-300–304 will make stacked PRs the default drain-cycle output. Without these patterns written down, they get re-derived every cycle.\n\n**Intended outcome:** the next stacked-PR drain has a runbook to read instead of a session to reconstruct, and the two new comment heuristics are loaded into every session by the existing SessionStart hook.\n\n**De-scoped on the user's instruction** (recorded so I don't reintroduce them on review):\n- Per-layer stop-and-wait completion ritual — engagement-specific, not a general workflow rule.\n- `docs/design-decisions.md §N` cross-reference clause — project-specific to drain-cycle, not transferrable.\n- PR-description iteration / Strunk wiring for prose deliverables — not in scope.\n\n## Scope\n\nTwo changes, both in `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/`. No other repos touched.\n\n### Change 1 — New rule module: `rules/graphite-stack-review.md`\n\nA self-contained ~60–80 line runbook for handling reviewer comments on a Graphite-stacked PR series. Style matches `rules/linear-workflow.md` (verified in exploration): second person, numbered procedure sections, backtick-wrapped commands inline, MCP-tool references where applicable.\n\nContent sections, in order:\n\n1. **Fetch the reviewer's comments.** `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/pulls/{N}/comments` for each PR in the stack. Quote the exact command form because it's the load-bearing primitive.\n2. **Triage by layer.** For each comment, identify which PR/layer is the right home — a comment on PR 3 about a line introduced in PR 1 still gets fixed in PR 1.\n3. **Amend bottom-up.** `gt checkout <bottom-branch>`, make the fix, `git commit --amend --no-edit`. Stay on that layer until the fix is committed.\n4. **Propagate with restack.** `gt restack` to walk parent SHAs upstream so every higher layer points at the new bottom-layer commit.\n5. **Force-push the stack.** `gt submit --stack` to push all amended layers in one shot. Verify with `gh pr view {N} --json headRefOid` that GitHub's PR head SHA matches the local branch head.\n6. **Recovery: Edit-tool \"file has been modified\".** Re-read the file, then re-apply the edit. Do not blind-retry the same Edit call — the file diverged and the old `old_string` no longer matches.\n\n### Change 2 — Extend `rules/GOVERNANCE.md`\n\nTwo single-bullet additions to the existing `Comments` section, alongside the existing no-ticket-IDs / no-fix-history-narration rules:\n\n- **Comment-as-smell.** If the only justification for a special-case branch or constant is a multi-line explanatory comment, prefer deleting the special case. A comment that explains *why this hack is needed* signals the design is wrong; remove the hack and the comment together.\n- **No `README §N` references from code comments.** Code comments must not point at README section numbers — the two artefacts evolve independently and the link rots silently. (References to other in-repo source files via path are fine; section numbers are not.)\n\nPlus a one-line pointer in the modules-index area of GOVERNANCE.md to `rules/graphite-stack-review.md`, in the same style as the existing `Read rules/linear-workflow.md for …` pointer.\n\n### Change 3 — Bump plugin version\n\nIncrement the `version` field in `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` so users who pick up updates get both changes in one bump.\n\n## Critical files\n\n- `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/graphite-stack-review.md` — **new** (~60–80 lines).\n- `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/GOVERNANCE.md` — **edit**: two new bullets in the `Comments` section, one new module pointer.\n- `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` — **edit**: bump version.\n\n## Existing patterns to reuse\n\n- **Module style:** match `rules/linear-workflow.md`'s register (numbered procedures, second person, inline backtick commands).\n- **GOVERNANCE.md indexing:** match the existing `Read rules/<file>.md for …` pointer pattern.\n- **MCP/CLI tool references:** match the inline backtick-wrapped style used in `linear-workflow.md` for `mcp__claude_ai_Linear__save_issue` — same pattern for `gh api`, `gt restack`, `gt submit --stack`.\n\n## Linear tracking\n\nTwo changes, total ~3 issues at most — well below the 5-issue initiative threshold per `rules/linear-workflow.md`. These go into the **Ops - bugs, maintenance, emergent** container as standalone issues, **not** a new initiative:\n\n- *\"Add Graphite stack-review runbook to workflow pack\"* (Change 1 + version bump).\n- *\"Extend GOVERNANCE.md Comments rule with comment-as-smell and no-README-section-refs\"* (Change 2).\n\nCould be folded into a single issue if the order is \"land them together\"; the split is for review-PR clarity, not delivery sequencing.\n\n**Scheduling:** wait until ABA-269–273 (the in-flight workflow-pack surgery / migration) is Done — that work is restructuring the pack and concurrent additions would conflict.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- `drain-cycle` changes — ABA-300–304 already plan the stacked-PR output feature; this plan does not touch them.\n- Editing the upstream `code-review-and-quality` marketplace skill — the heuristics live in the user's own pack instead.\n- Per-layer stop-and-wait ritual (de-scoped).\n- `docs/design-decisions.md §N` cross-reference clause (de-scoped).\n- PR-description iteration / Strunk wiring (de-scoped).\n\n## Verification\n\nAfter both changes land:\n\n1. **File exists and is indexed.**\n   - `ls /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/graphite-stack-review.md` succeeds.\n   - `grep -n \"graphite-stack-review\" /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/GOVERNANCE.md` returns exactly 1 line.\n2. **SessionStart injects the new bullets.**\n   - Run `bash /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/hooks/session-start.sh` and inspect the JSON `message` field. Confirm both new strings appear: \"comment-as-smell\" (or equivalent verbatim from the bullet) and \"README §\" (or the verbatim no-section-refs wording).\n3. **Plugin version bumped.**\n   - `jq -r .version /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` shows a higher value than the current main.\n4. **Tabletop walkthrough against ABA-299.**\n   - Open the (now-merged) ABA-299 stack and run each step of the new runbook against what actually happened. Every command in the runbook should match a command actually used during this run's review-comment handling — if any doesn't, either the runbook is wrong or the original move was ad-hoc.\n"
}

## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Plan: capture stacked-PR learnings into agent-skills-workflow

## Context

ABA-299 was delivered as a 4-PR Graphite stack rather than the usual push-to-main. That run surfaced two reusable patterns the workflow pack does not yet capture:

1. **Handling reviewer comments across a stacked PR series.** Fetching comments via `gh api`, triaging which layer owns each one, amending bottom-up with `git commit --amend`, propagating with `gt restack`, and force-pushing the stack with `gt submit --stack`.
2. **Two code-comment heuristics observed by the reviewer.** "Comment-as-smell" (when the only justification for a special case is a multi-line explanatory comment, prefer deleting the special case — canonical example: the `_INT_ONLY_FIELDS` constant in `drain-cycle/drain_cycle/limits.py` from this run). And "no `README §N` references from code comments" (a code comment that says `see README §3` couples two artefacts that evolve independently; the link rots silently when README is restructured).

ABA-300–304 will make stacked PRs the default drain-cycle output. Without these patterns written down, they get re-derived every cycle.

**Intended outcome:** the next stacked-PR drain has a runbook to read instead of a session to reconstruct, and the two new comment heuristics are loaded into every session by the existing SessionStart hook.

**De-scoped on the user's instruction** (recorded so I don't reintroduce them on review):
- Per-layer stop-and-wait completion ritual — engagement-specific, not a general workflow rule.
- `docs/design-decisions.md §N` cross-reference clause — project-specific to drain-cycle, not transferrable.
- PR-description iteration / Strunk wiring for prose deliverables — not in scope.

## Scope

Two changes, both in `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/`. No other repos touched.

### Change 1 — New rule module: `rules/graphite-stack-review.md`

A self-contained ~60–80 line runbook for handling reviewer comments on a Graphite-stacked PR series. Style matches `rules/linear-workflow.md` (verified in exploration): second person, numbered procedure sections, backtick-wrapped commands inline, MCP-tool references where applicable.

Content sections, in order:

1. **Fetch the reviewer's comments.** `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/pulls/{N}/comments` for each PR in the stack. Quote the exact command form because it's the load-bearing primitive.
2. **Triage by layer.** For each comment, identify which PR/layer is the right home — a comment on PR 3 about a line introduced in PR 1 still gets fixed in PR 1.
3. **Amend bottom-up.** `gt checkout <bottom-branch>`, make the fix, `git commit --amend --no-edit`. Stay on that layer until the fix is committed.
4. **Propagate with restack.** `gt restack` to walk parent SHAs upstream so every higher layer points at the new bottom-layer commit.
5. **Force-push the stack.** `gt submit --stack` to push all amended layers in one shot. Verify with `gh pr view {N} --json headRefOid` that GitHub's PR head SHA matches the local branch head.

### Change 2 — Extend `rules/GOVERNANCE.md`

Two single-bullet additions to the existing `Comments` section, alongside the existing no-ticket-IDs / no-fix-history-narration rules:

- **Comment-as-smell.** If the only justification for a special-case branch or constant is a multi-line explanatory comment, prefer deleting the special case. A comment that explains *why this hack is needed* signals the design is wrong; remove the hack and the comment together.
- **No `README §N` references from code comments.** Code comments must not point at README section numbers — the two artefacts evolve independently and the link rots silently. (References to other in-repo source files via path are fine; section numbers are not.)

Plus a one-line pointer in the modules-index area of GOVERNANCE.md to `rules/graphite-stack-review.md`, in the same style as the existing `Read rules/linear-workflow.md for …` pointer.

### Change 3 — Bump plugin version

Increment the `version` field in `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` so users who pick up updates get both changes in one bump.

## Critical files

- `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/graphite-stack-review.md` — **new** (~60–80 lines).
- `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/GOVERNANCE.md` — **edit**: two new bullets in the `Comments` section, one new module pointer.
- `/Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` — **edit**: bump version.

## Existing patterns to reuse

- **Module style:** match `rules/linear-workflow.md`'s register (numbered procedures, second person, inline backtick commands).
- **GOVERNANCE.md indexing:** match the existing `Read rules/<file>.md for …` pointer pattern.
- **MCP/CLI tool references:** match the inline backtick-wrapped style used in `linear-workflow.md` for `mcp__claude_ai_Linear__save_issue` — same pattern for `gh api`, `gt restack`, `gt submit --stack`.

## Linear tracking

Two changes, total ~3 issues at most — well below the 5-issue initiative threshold per `rules/linear-workflow.md`. These go into the **Ops - bugs, maintenance, emergent** container as standalone issues, **not** a new initiative:

- *"Add Graphite stack-review runbook to workflow pack"* (Change 1 + version bump).
- *"Extend GOVERNANCE.md Comments rule with comment-as-smell and no-README-section-refs"* (Change 2).

Could be folded into a single issue if the order is "land them together"; the split is for review-PR clarity, not delivery sequencing.

**Scheduling:** wait until ABA-269–273 (the in-flight workflow-pack surgery / migration) is Done — that work is restructuring the pack and concurrent additions would conflict.

## Out of scope

- `drain-cycle` changes — ABA-300–304 already plan the stacked-PR output feature; this plan does not touch them.
- Editing the upstream `code-review-and-quality` marketplace skill — the heuristics live in the user's own pack instead.
- Per-layer stop-and-wait ritual (de-scoped).
- `docs/design-decisions.md §N` cross-reference clause (de-scoped).
- PR-description iteration / Strunk wiring (de-scoped).

## Verification

After both changes land:

1. **File exists and is indexed.**
   - `ls /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/graphite-stack-review.md` succeeds.
   - `grep -n "graphite-stack-review" /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/rules/GOVERNANCE.md` returns exactly 1 line.
2. **SessionStart injects the new bullets.**
   - Run `bash /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/hooks/session-start.sh` and inspect the JSON `message` field. Confirm both new strings appear: "comment-as-smell" (or equivalent verbatim from the bullet) and "README §" (or the verbatim no-section-refs wording).
3. **Plugin version bumped.**
   - `jq -r .version /Users/anton/src/agent-skills-workflow/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` shows a higher value than the current main.
4. **Tabletop walkthrough against ABA-299.**
   - Open the (now-merged) ABA-299 stack and run each step of the new runbook against what actually happened. Every command in the runbook should match a command actually used during this run's review-comment handling — if any doesn't, either the runbook is wrong or the original move was ad-hoc.

```
